\begin {align}f'(2) &= \pause \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 2} \dfrac {f(x) - f(2)}{x - 2} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 2} \dfrac {x^2 - 2^2}{x - 2} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 2} \dfrac {(x - 2)(x + 2)}{x - 2} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 2} x + 2 \pause = 2 + 2 = 4 \pause \end {align}

\begin {align}f'(4) &= \pause \lim \limits _{x\rightarrow 4} \dfrac {f(x) - f(4)}{x - 4} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 4} \dfrac {\sqrt {x} - \sqrt {4}}{x - 4} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 4} \dfrac {\sqrt {x} + \sqrt {4}}{\sqrt {x} + \sqrt {4}} \dfrac {\sqrt {x} - \sqrt {4}}{x - 4} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 4} \dfrac {(\sqrt {x})^2 - (\sqrt {4})^2}{(x - 4)(\sqrt {x} + \sqrt {4})} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 4} \dfrac {x - 4}{(x - 4)(\sqrt {x} + \sqrt {4})} \pause \\ &= \lim \limits _{x \rightarrow 4} \dfrac {1}{\sqrt {x} + \sqrt {4}} \pause = \dfrac {1}{\sqrt {4} + \sqrt {4}} \pause = \dfrac {1}{4} \pause \end {align}

\begin {align}\dfrac {d}{dx} \tan (x) &= \dfrac {d}{dx} \dfrac {\sin (x)}{\cos (x)} \\ \pause &= \dfrac {\left (\dfrac {d}{dx}\sin (x)\right )\cdot \cos (x) - \sin (x) \cdot \left (\dfrac {d}{dx} \cos (x)\right )}{\cos (x)^2} \\ \pause &= \dfrac {\cos (x) \cdot \cos (x) - \sin (x) \cdot (- \sin (x))}{\cos (x)^2} \\ \pause &= \dfrac {\cos (x)^2 + \sin (x)^2}{\cos (x)^2} \\ \pause &= \dfrac {1}{\cos (x)^2} \pause = \sec (x)^2\end {align}

\mml 1097

\begin {align}\dfrac {d}{dx} \arcsin (x) &= \dfrac {1}{\left (\dfrac {d}{du}\sin (u)\right )\mid _{u = \arcsin (x)}} \pause = \dfrac {1}{(\cos (u))\mid _{u = \arcsin (x)}} \\ &= \cos (\arcsin (x)) \pause = \dfrac {1}{\sqrt {1 - x^2}} \\ \pause \dfrac {d}{dx} \arccos (x) &= \dfrac {1}{-\sin (\arccos (x))} \pause = -\dfrac {1}{\sqrt {1 - x^2}} \\ \pause \dfrac {d}{dx} \arctan (x) &= \dfrac {1}{\sec ^2(\arctan (x))} \pause = \dfrac {1}{1 + x^2}\end {align}
